+// Include row overhead in the partition weight, so empty rows are also balanced.
+// Whole rows retain the original floating-point summation order.
+void partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters, const index_t numRows,
+                   std::vector<index_t>& rowCuts, std::vector<index_t>& itemCuts) {
+    const size_t ranks = rowCuts.size() - 1;
+    const uint64_t totalWork = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[numRows]) + numRows;
+    for (size_t p = 1; p < ranks; ++p) {
+        const uint64_t target = totalWork * p / ranks;
+        // If there are enough rows, give every rank at least one.
+        const index_t first = rowCuts[p - 1] + (numRows >= ranks ? 1 : 0);
+        const index_t last = numRows >= ranks ? numRows - (ranks - p) : numRows;
+        index_t low = first;
+        index_t high = last;
+        while (low < high) {
+            const index_t mid = low + (high - low) / 2;
+            const uint64_t work = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid;
+            if (work < target) {
+                low = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                high = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        if (low > first) {
+            const uint64_t before = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[low - 1]) + low - 1;
+            const uint64_t after = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[low]) + low;
+            if (after >= target && target - before <= after - target) {
+                --low;
+            }
+        }
+        rowCuts[p] = low;
+    }
+    rowCuts[ranks] = numRows;
+    for (size_t p = 0; p <= ranks; ++p) {
+        itemCuts[p] = rowDelimiters[rowCuts[p]];
+    }
+}
+
+// Chunk transfers at MPI's int count/displacement limit. The normal case uses
+// one collective, while large CSR arrays do not overflow MPI counts.
+template <typename T>
+void broadcastVector(std::vector<T>& data, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < data.size();) {
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min(data.size() - offset, size_t{INT_MAX}));
+        MPI_Bcast(data.data() + offset, count, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset += count;
+    }
+}
+
+void sliceWindow(const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const uint64_t offset,
+                 const uint64_t end, std::vector<int>& counts, std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < counts.size(); ++p) {
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[p], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[p + 1], end);
+        counts[p] = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        displacements[p] = last > first ? static_cast<int>(first - offset) : 0;
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void scatterSlices(std::vector<T>& global, std::vector<T>& local,
+                   const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const int rank, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    const size_t ranks = cuts.size() - 1;
+    if (ranks == 1) {
+        local = std::move(global);
+        return;
+    }
+    local.resize(static_cast<size_t>(cuts[rank + 1]) - cuts[rank]);
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    T dummy{};
+    for (uint64_t offset = 0; offset < cuts.back();) {
+        const uint64_t end = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts.back(), offset + INT_MAX);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            sliceWindow(cuts, offset, end, counts, displacements);
+        }
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[rank], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[rank + 1], end);
+        const int count = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        T* receive = count ? local.data() + (first - cuts[rank]) : &dummy;
+        MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? global.data() + offset : nullptr, counts.data(),
+                     displacements.data(), datatype, receive, count, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset = end;
+    }
+    // Keep only the rank's CSR block after distribution, including on rank 0.
+    std::vector<T>().swap(global);
+}
+
+void gatherOutput(std::vector<double>& local, std::vector<double>& global,
+                  const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const int rank) {
+    const size_t ranks = cuts.size() - 1;
+    if (ranks == 1) {
+        global = std::move(local);
+        return;
+    }
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        global.resize(cuts.back());
+    }
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    double dummy = 0.0;
+    for (uint64_t offset = 0; offset < cuts.back();) {
+        const uint64_t end = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts.back(), offset + INT_MAX);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            sliceWindow(cuts, offset, end, counts, displacements);
+        }
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[rank], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[rank + 1], end);
+        const int count = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        const double* send = count ? local.data() + (first - cuts[rank]) : &dummy;
+        MPI_Gatherv(send, count, MPI_DOUBLE, rank == 0 ? global.data() + offset : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset = end;
+    }
+}
+
+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    errno = 0;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (text[0] == '-' || end == text || *end != '\0' || errno == ERANGE ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

/usr/bin/clang-format
// Purpose:
//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format
//
// Arguments:
//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix
//   cols: array of column indices for each element
//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;
//                  last element is the index one past the last element
//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication
//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix
//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation
//
// ****************************************************************************
void spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,
             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {
    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {
        double t = 0.0;
        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {
            const auto col = cols[j];
            t += val[j] * vec[col];
        }
        out[i] = t;
    }
}

+// Include row overhead in the partition weight, so empty rows are also balanced.
+// Whole rows retain the original floating-point summation order.
+void partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters, const index_t numRows,
+                   std::vector<index_t>& rowCuts, std::vector<index_t>& itemCuts) {
+    const size_t ranks = rowCuts.size() - 1;
+    const uint64_t totalWork = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[numRows]) + numRows;
+    for (size_t p = 1; p < ranks; ++p) {
+        const uint64_t target = totalWork * p / ranks;
+        // If there are enough rows, give every rank at least one.
+        const index_t first = rowCuts[p - 1] + (numRows >= ranks ? 1 : 0);
+        const index_t last = numRows >= ranks ? numRows - (ranks - p) : numRows;
+        index_t low = first;
+        index_t high = last;
+        while (low < high) {
+            const index_t mid = low + (high - low) / 2;
+            const uint64_t work = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid;
+            if (work < target) {
+                low = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                high = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        if (low > first) {
+            const uint64_t before = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[low - 1]) + low - 1;
+            const uint64_t after = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[low]) + low;
+            if (after >= target && target - before <= after - target) {
+                --low;
+            }
+        }
+        rowCuts[p] = low;
+    }
+    rowCuts[ranks] = numRows;
+    for (size_t p = 0; p <= ranks; ++p) {
+        itemCuts[p] = rowDelimiters[rowCuts[p]];
+    }
+}
+
+// Chunk transfers at MPI's int count/displacement limit. The normal case uses
+// one collective, while large CSR arrays do not overflow MPI counts.
+template <typename T>
+void broadcastVector(std::vector<T>& data, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < data.size();) {
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min(data.size() - offset, size_t{INT_MAX}));
+        MPI_Bcast(data.data() + offset, count, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset += count;
+    }
+}
+
+void sliceWindow(const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const uint64_t offset,
+                 const uint64_t end, std::vector<int>& counts, std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < counts.size(); ++p) {
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[p], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[p + 1], end);
+        counts[p] = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        displacements[p] = last > first ? static_cast<int>(first - offset) : 0;
+    }
+}

+template <typename T>
+void scatterSlices(std::vector<T>& global, std::vector<T>& local,
+                   const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const int rank, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    const size_t ranks = cuts.size() - 1;

+        return;

+    local.resize(static_cast<size_t>(cuts[rank + 1]) - cuts[rank]);

+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());

+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[rank + 1], end);
+        const int count = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        T* receive = count ? local.data() + (first - cuts[rank]) : &dummy;
+        MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? global.data() + offset : nullptr, counts.data(),
+                     displacements.data(), datatype, receive, count, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset = end;
+    }
+    // Keep only the rank's CSR block after distribution, including on rank 0.
+    std::vector<T>().swap(global);
+}

+void gatherOutput(std::vector<double>& local, std::vector<double>& global,
+                  const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const int rank) {
+    const size_t ranks = cuts.size() - 1;
+    if (ranks == 1) {
+        global = std::move(local);
+        return;
+    }
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        global.resize(cuts.back());

+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);

+        const uint64_t end = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts.back(), offset + INT_MAX);
+        if (rank == 0) {

+        }
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[rank], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[rank + 1], end);
+        const int count = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        const double* send = count ? local.data() + (first - cuts[rank]) : &dummy;
+        MPI_Gatherv(send, count, MPI_DOUBLE, rank == 0 ? global.data() + offset : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset = end;
+    }
+}

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    errno = 0;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (text[0] == '-' || end == text || *end != '\0' || errno == ERANGE ||

+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;

kernel = '''
// Interleave four rows to expose independent accumulations without changing
// the order of additions within any row.
void spmvLocal(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,
               const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {
    index_t i = 0;
    for (; dim - i >= 4; i += 4) {
        index_t j0 = rowDelimiters[i];
        index_t j1 = rowDelimiters[i + 1];
        index_t j2 = rowDelimiters[i + 2];
        index_t j3 = rowDelimiters[i + 3];
        const index_t end0 = j1;
        const index_t end1 = j2;
        const index_t end2 = j3;
        const index_t end3 = rowDelimiters[i + 4];
        const index_t common = std::min(std::min(end0 - j0, end1 - j1),
                                        std::min(end2 - j2, end3 - j3));
        const index_t commonEnd = j0 + common;
        double t0 = 0.0;
        double t1 = 0.0;
        double t2 = 0.0;
        double t3 = 0.0;
        for (; j0 < commonEnd; ++j0, ++j1, ++j2, ++j3) {
            t0 += val[j0] * vec[cols[j0]];
            t1 += val[j1] * vec[cols[j1]];
            t2 += val[j2] * vec[cols[j2]];
            t3 += val[j3] * vec[cols[j3]];
        }
        for (; j0 < end0; ++j0) t0 += val[j0] * vec[cols[j0]];
        for (; j1 < end1; ++j1) t1 += val[j1] * vec[cols[j1]];
        for (; j2 < end2; ++j2) t2 += val[j2] * vec[cols[j2]];
        for (; j3 < end3; ++j3) t3 += val[j3] * vec[cols[j3]];
        out[i] = t0;
        out[i + 1] = t1;
        out[i + 2] = t2;
        out[i + 3] = t3;
    }
    if (i < dim) {
        spmvCpu(val, cols, rowDelimiters + i, vec, dim - i, out + i);
    }
}
'''

+// Include row overhead in the partition weight, so empty rows are also balanced.
+// Whole rows retain the original floating-point summation order.
+void partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters, const index_t numRows,
+                   std::vector<index_t>& rowCuts, std::vector<index_t>& itemCuts) {
+    const size_t ranks = rowCuts.size() - 1;
+    const uint64_t totalWork = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[numRows]) + numRows;
+    for (size_t p = 1; p < ranks; ++p) {
+        const uint64_t target = totalWork * p / ranks;
+        // If there are enough rows, give every rank at least one.
+        const index_t first = rowCuts[p - 1] + (numRows >= ranks ? 1 : 0);
+        const index_t last = numRows >= ranks ? numRows - (ranks - p) : numRows;
+        index_t low = first;
+        index_t high = last;
+        while (low < high) {
+            const index_t mid = low + (high - low) / 2;
+            const uint64_t work = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid;
+            if (work < target) {
+                low = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                high = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        if (low > first) {
+            const uint64_t before = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[low - 1]) + low - 1;
+            const uint64_t after = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[low]) + low;
+            if (after >= target && target - before <= after - target) {
+                --low;
+            }
+        }
+        rowCuts[p] = low;
+    }
+    rowCuts[ranks] = numRows;
+    for (size_t p = 0; p <= ranks; ++p) {
+        itemCuts[p] = rowDelimiters[rowCuts[p]];
+    }
+}
+
+// Chunk transfers at MPI's int count/displacement limit. The normal case uses
+// one collective, while large CSR arrays do not overflow MPI counts.
+template <typename T>
+void broadcastVector(std::vector<T>& data, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < data.size();) {
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min(data.size() - offset, size_t{INT_MAX}));
+        MPI_Bcast(data.data() + offset, count, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset += count;
+    }
+}
+
+void sliceWindow(const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const uint64_t offset,
+                 const uint64_t end, std::vector<int>& counts, std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < counts.size(); ++p) {
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[p], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[p + 1], end);
+        counts[p] = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        displacements[p] = last > first ? static_cast<int>(first - offset) : 0;
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void scatterSlices(std::vector<T>& global, std::vector<T>& local,
+                   const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const int rank, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    const size_t ranks = cuts.size() - 1;
+    if (ranks == 1) {
+        local = std::move(global);
+        return;
+    }
+    local.resize(static_cast<size_t>(cuts[rank + 1]) - cuts[rank]);
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    T dummy{};
+    for (uint64_t offset = 0; offset < cuts.back();) {
+        const uint64_t end = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts.back(), offset + INT_MAX);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            sliceWindow(cuts, offset, end, counts, displacements);
+        }
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[rank], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[rank + 1], end);
+        const int count = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        T* receive = count ? local.data() + (first - cuts[rank]) : &dummy;
+        MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? global.data() + offset : nullptr, counts.data(),
+                     displacements.data(), datatype, receive, count, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset = end;
+    }
+    // Keep only the rank's CSR block after distribution, including on rank 0.
+    std::vector<T>().swap(global);
+}
+
+void gatherOutput(std::vector<double>& local, std::vector<double>& global,
+                  const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const int rank) {
+    const size_t ranks = cuts.size() - 1;
+    if (ranks == 1) {
+        global = std::move(local);
+        return;
+    }
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        global.resize(cuts.back());
+    }
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    double dummy = 0.0;
+    for (uint64_t offset = 0; offset < cuts.back();) {
+        const uint64_t end = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts.back(), offset + INT_MAX);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            sliceWindow(cuts, offset, end, counts, displacements);
+        }
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[rank], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[rank + 1], end);
+        const int count = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        const double* send = count ? local.data() + (first - cuts[rank]) : &dummy;
+        MPI_Gatherv(send, count, MPI_DOUBLE, rank == 0 ? global.data() + offset : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset = end;
+    }
+}
+
+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    errno = 0;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (text[0] == '-' || end == text || *end != '\0' || errno == ERANGE ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

+// Include row overhead in the partition weight, so empty rows are also balanced.
+// Whole rows retain the original floating-point summation order.
+void partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters, const index_t numRows,
+                   std::vector<index_t>& rowCuts, std::vector<index_t>& itemCuts) {
+    const size_t ranks = rowCuts.size() - 1;
+    const uint64_t totalWork = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[numRows]) + numRows;
+    for (size_t p = 1; p < ranks; ++p) {
+        const uint64_t target = totalWork * p / ranks;
+        // If there are enough rows, give every rank at least one.
+        const index_t first = rowCuts[p - 1] + (numRows >= ranks ? 1 : 0);
+        const index_t last = numRows >= ranks ? numRows - (ranks - p) : numRows;
+        index_t low = first;
+        index_t high = last;
+        while (low < high) {
+            const index_t mid = low + (high - low) / 2;
+            const uint64_t work = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid;
+            if (work < target) {
+                low = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                high = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        if (low > first) {
+            const uint64_t before = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[low - 1]) + low - 1;
+            const uint64_t after = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[low]) + low;
+            if (after >= target && target - before <= after - target) {
+                --low;
+            }
+        }
+        rowCuts[p] = low;
+    }
+    rowCuts[ranks] = numRows;
+    for (size_t p = 0; p <= ranks; ++p) {
+        itemCuts[p] = rowDelimiters[rowCuts[p]];
+    }
+}
+
+// Chunk transfers at MPI's int count/displacement limit. The normal case uses
+// one collective, while large CSR arrays do not overflow MPI counts.
+template <typename T>
+void broadcastVector(std::vector<T>& data, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < data.size();) {
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min(data.size() - offset, size_t{INT_MAX}));
+        MPI_Bcast(data.data() + offset, count, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset += count;
+    }
+}
+
+void sliceWindow(const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const uint64_t offset,
+                 const uint64_t end, std::vector<int>& counts, std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < counts.size(); ++p) {
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[p], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[p + 1], end);
+        counts[p] = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        displacements[p] = last > first ? static_cast<int>(first - offset) : 0;
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void scatterSlices(std::vector<T>& global, std::vector<T>& local,
+                   const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const int rank, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    const size_t ranks = cuts.size() - 1;
+    if (ranks == 1) {
+        local = std::move(global);
+        return;
+    }
+    local.resize(static_cast<size_t>(cuts[rank + 1]) - cuts[rank]);
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    T dummy{};
+    for (uint64_t offset = 0; offset < cuts.back();) {
+        const uint64_t end = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts.back(), offset + INT_MAX);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            sliceWindow(cuts, offset, end, counts, displacements);
+        }
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[rank], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[rank + 1], end);
+        const int count = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        T* receive = count ? local.data() + (first - cuts[rank]) : &dummy;
+        MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? global.data() + offset : nullptr, counts.data(),
+                     displacements.data(), datatype, receive, count, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset = end;
+    }
+    // Keep only the rank's CSR block after distribution, including on rank 0.
+    std::vector<T>().swap(global);
+}
+
+void gatherOutput(std::vector<double>& local, std::vector<double>& global,
+                  const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const int rank) {
+    const size_t ranks = cuts.size() - 1;
+    if (ranks == 1) {
+        global = std::move(local);
+        return;
+    }
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        global.resize(cuts.back());
+    }
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    double dummy = 0.0;
+    for (uint64_t offset = 0; offset < cuts.back();) {
+        const uint64_t end = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts.back(), offset + INT_MAX);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            sliceWindow(cuts, offset, end, counts, displacements);
+        }
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[rank], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[rank + 1], end);
+        const int count = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        const double* send = count ? local.data() + (first - cuts[rank]) : &dummy;
+        MPI_Gatherv(send, count, MPI_DOUBLE, rank == 0 ? global.data() + offset : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset = end;
+    }
+}
+
+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    errno = 0;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (text[0] == '-' || end == text || *end != '\0' || errno == ERANGE ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

codex
So far, the MPI version matches the original exactly for empty matrices, dense matrices, and runs with more ranks than rows. Initial local timings also scale well through eight ranks. I’m checking a local kernel optimization that could improve throughput while preserving each row’s addition order.

import re, subprocess
paths = ['/tmp/spmv-mpi-check.w62WK5/build/spmv','/tmp/spmv-mpi-check.w62WK5/spmv-blocked']

+// Include row overhead in the partition weight, so empty rows are also balanced.
+// Whole rows retain the original floating-point summation order.
+void partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters, const index_t numRows,
+                   std::vector<index_t>& rowCuts, std::vector<index_t>& itemCuts) {
+    const size_t ranks = rowCuts.size() - 1;
+    const uint64_t totalWork = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[numRows]) + numRows;
+    for (size_t p = 1; p < ranks; ++p) {
+        const uint64_t target = totalWork * p / ranks;
+        // If there are enough rows, give every rank at least one.
+        const index_t first = rowCuts[p - 1] + (numRows >= ranks ? 1 : 0);
+        const index_t last = numRows >= ranks ? numRows - (ranks - p) : numRows;
+        index_t low = first;
+        index_t high = last;
+        while (low < high) {
+            const index_t mid = low + (high - low) / 2;
+            const uint64_t work = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid;
+            if (work < target) {
+                low = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                high = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        if (low > first) {
+            const uint64_t before = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[low - 1]) + low - 1;
+            const uint64_t after = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[low]) + low;
+            if (after >= target && target - before <= after - target) {
+                --low;
+            }
+        }
+        rowCuts[p] = low;
+    }
+    rowCuts[ranks] = numRows;
+    for (size_t p = 0; p <= ranks; ++p) {
+        itemCuts[p] = rowDelimiters[rowCuts[p]];
+    }
+}
+
+// Chunk transfers at MPI's int count/displacement limit. The normal case uses
+// one collective, while large CSR arrays do not overflow MPI counts.
+template <typename T>
+void broadcastVector(std::vector<T>& data, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < data.size();) {
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min(data.size() - offset, size_t{INT_MAX}));
+        MPI_Bcast(data.data() + offset, count, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset += count;
+    }
+}
+
+void sliceWindow(const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const uint64_t offset,
+                 const uint64_t end, std::vector<int>& counts, std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < counts.size(); ++p) {
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[p], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[p + 1], end);
+        counts[p] = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        displacements[p] = last > first ? static_cast<int>(first - offset) : 0;
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void scatterSlices(std::vector<T>& global, std::vector<T>& local,
+                   const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const int rank, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    const size_t ranks = cuts.size() - 1;
+    if (ranks == 1) {
+        local = std::move(global);
+        return;
+    }
+    local.resize(static_cast<size_t>(cuts[rank + 1]) - cuts[rank]);
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    T dummy{};
+    for (uint64_t offset = 0; offset < cuts.back();) {
+        const uint64_t end = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts.back(), offset + INT_MAX);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            sliceWindow(cuts, offset, end, counts, displacements);
+        }
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[rank], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[rank + 1], end);
+        const int count = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        T* receive = count ? local.data() + (first - cuts[rank]) : &dummy;
+        MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? global.data() + offset : nullptr, counts.data(),
+                     displacements.data(), datatype, receive, count, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset = end;
+    }
+    // Keep only the rank's CSR block after distribution, including on rank 0.
+    std::vector<T>().swap(global);
+}
+
+void gatherOutput(std::vector<double>& local, std::vector<double>& global,
+                  const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const int rank) {
+    const size_t ranks = cuts.size() - 1;
+    if (ranks == 1) {
+        global = std::move(local);
+        return;
+    }
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        global.resize(cuts.back());
+    }
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    double dummy = 0.0;
+    for (uint64_t offset = 0; offset < cuts.back();) {
+        const uint64_t end = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts.back(), offset + INT_MAX);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            sliceWindow(cuts, offset, end, counts, displacements);
+        }
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[rank], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[rank + 1], end);
+        const int count = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        const double* send = count ? local.data() + (first - cuts[rank]) : &dummy;
+        MPI_Gatherv(send, count, MPI_DOUBLE, rank == 0 ? global.data() + offset : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset = end;
+    }
+}
+
+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    errno = 0;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (text[0] == '-' || end == text || *end != '\0' || errno == ERANGE ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

+// Include row overhead in the partition weight, so empty rows are also balanced.
+// Whole rows retain the original floating-point summation order.
+void partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters, const index_t numRows,
+                   std::vector<index_t>& rowCuts, std::vector<index_t>& itemCuts) {
+    const size_t ranks = rowCuts.size() - 1;
+    const uint64_t totalWork = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[numRows]) + numRows;
+    for (size_t p = 1; p < ranks; ++p) {
+        const uint64_t target = totalWork * p / ranks;
+        // If there are enough rows, give every rank at least one.
+        const index_t first = rowCuts[p - 1] + (numRows >= ranks ? 1 : 0);
+        const index_t last = numRows >= ranks ? numRows - (ranks - p) : numRows;
+        index_t low = first;
+        index_t high = last;
+        while (low < high) {
+            const index_t mid = low + (high - low) / 2;
+            const uint64_t work = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid;
+            if (work < target) {
+                low = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                high = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        if (low > first) {
+            const uint64_t before = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[low - 1]) + low - 1;
+            const uint64_t after = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[low]) + low;
+            if (after >= target && target - before <= after - target) {
+                --low;
+            }
+        }
+        rowCuts[p] = low;
+    }
+    rowCuts[ranks] = numRows;
+    for (size_t p = 0; p <= ranks; ++p) {
+        itemCuts[p] = rowDelimiters[rowCuts[p]];
+    }
+}

+// Chunk transfers at MPI's int count/displacement limit. The normal case uses
+// one collective, while large CSR arrays do not overflow MPI counts.
+template <typename T>
+void broadcastVector(std::vector<T>& data, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < data.size();) {
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min(data.size() - offset, size_t{INT_MAX}));
+        MPI_Bcast(data.data() + offset, count, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset += count;
+    }
+}

+void sliceWindow(const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const uint64_t offset,
+                 const uint64_t end, std::vector<int>& counts, std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < counts.size(); ++p) {
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[p], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[p + 1], end);
+        counts[p] = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        displacements[p] = last > first ? static_cast<int>(first - offset) : 0;
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void scatterSlices(std::vector<T>& global, std::vector<T>& local,
+                   const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const int rank, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    const size_t ranks = cuts.size() - 1;
+    if (ranks == 1) {
+        local = std::move(global);
+        return;
+    }
+    local.resize(static_cast<size_t>(cuts[rank + 1]) - cuts[rank]);
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    T dummy{};
+    for (uint64_t offset = 0; offset < cuts.back();) {
+        const uint64_t end = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts.back(), offset + INT_MAX);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            sliceWindow(cuts, offset, end, counts, displacements);
+        }
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[rank], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[rank + 1], end);
+        const int count = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        T* receive = count ? local.data() + (first - cuts[rank]) : &dummy;
+        MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? global.data() + offset : nullptr, counts.data(),
+                     displacements.data(), datatype, receive, count, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset = end;
+    }
+    // Keep only the rank's CSR block after distribution, including on rank 0.
+    std::vector<T>().swap(global);
+}
+
+void gatherOutput(std::vector<double>& local, std::vector<double>& global,
+                  const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const int rank) {
+    const size_t ranks = cuts.size() - 1;
+    if (ranks == 1) {
+        global = std::move(local);
+        return;
+    }
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        global.resize(cuts.back());
+    }
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    double dummy = 0.0;
+    for (uint64_t offset = 0; offset < cuts.back();) {
+        const uint64_t end = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts.back(), offset + INT_MAX);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            sliceWindow(cuts, offset, end, counts, displacements);
+        }
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[rank], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[rank + 1], end);
+        const int count = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        const double* send = count ? local.data() + (first - cuts[rank]) : &dummy;
+        MPI_Gatherv(send, count, MPI_DOUBLE, rank == 0 ? global.data() + offset : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset = end;
+    }
+}
+
+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    errno = 0;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (text[0] == '-' || end == text || *end != '\0' || errno == ERANGE ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

+// Include row overhead in the partition weight, so empty rows are also balanced.
+// Whole rows retain the original floating-point summation order.
+void partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters, const index_t numRows,
+                   std::vector<index_t>& rowCuts, std::vector<index_t>& itemCuts) {
+    const size_t ranks = rowCuts.size() - 1;
+    const uint64_t totalWork = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[numRows]) + numRows;
+    for (size_t p = 1; p < ranks; ++p) {
+        const uint64_t target = totalWork * p / ranks;
+        // If there are enough rows, give every rank at least one.
+        const index_t first = rowCuts[p - 1] + (numRows >= ranks ? 1 : 0);
+        const index_t last = numRows >= ranks ? numRows - (ranks - p) : numRows;
+        index_t low = first;
+        index_t high = last;
+        while (low < high) {
+            const index_t mid = low + (high - low) / 2;
+            const uint64_t work = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid;
+            if (work < target) {
+                low = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                high = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        if (low > first) {
+            const uint64_t before = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[low - 1]) + low - 1;
+            const uint64_t after = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[low]) + low;
+            if (after >= target && target - before <= after - target) {
+                --low;
+            }
+        }
+        rowCuts[p] = low;
+    }
+    rowCuts[ranks] = numRows;
+    for (size_t p = 0; p <= ranks; ++p) {
+        itemCuts[p] = rowDelimiters[rowCuts[p]];
+    }
+}
+
+// Chunk transfers at MPI's int count/displacement limit. The normal case uses
+// one collective, while large CSR arrays do not overflow MPI counts.
+template <typename T>
+void broadcastVector(std::vector<T>& data, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < data.size();) {
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min(data.size() - offset, size_t{INT_MAX}));
+        MPI_Bcast(data.data() + offset, count, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset += count;
+    }
+}
+
+void sliceWindow(const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const uint64_t offset,
+                 const uint64_t end, std::vector<int>& counts, std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < counts.size(); ++p) {
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[p], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[p + 1], end);
+        counts[p] = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        displacements[p] = last > first ? static_cast<int>(first - offset) : 0;
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void scatterSlices(std::vector<T>& global, std::vector<T>& local,
+                   const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const int rank, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    const size_t ranks = cuts.size() - 1;
+    if (ranks == 1) {
+        local = std::move(global);
+        return;
+    }
+    local.resize(static_cast<size_t>(cuts[rank + 1]) - cuts[rank]);
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    T dummy{};
+    for (uint64_t offset = 0; offset < cuts.back();) {
+        const uint64_t end = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts.back(), offset + INT_MAX);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            sliceWindow(cuts, offset, end, counts, displacements);
+        }
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[rank], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[rank + 1], end);
+        const int count = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        T* receive = count ? local.data() + (first - cuts[rank]) : &dummy;
+        MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? global.data() + offset : nullptr, counts.data(),
+                     displacements.data(), datatype, receive, count, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset = end;
+    }
+    // Keep only the rank's CSR block after distribution, including on rank 0.
+    std::vector<T>().swap(global);
+}
+
+void gatherOutput(std::vector<double>& local, std::vector<double>& global,
+                  const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const int rank) {
+    const size_t ranks = cuts.size() - 1;
+    if (ranks == 1) {
+        global = std::move(local);
+        return;
+    }
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        global.resize(cuts.back());
+    }
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    double dummy = 0.0;
+    for (uint64_t offset = 0; offset < cuts.back();) {
+        const uint64_t end = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts.back(), offset + INT_MAX);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            sliceWindow(cuts, offset, end, counts, displacements);
+        }
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[rank], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[rank + 1], end);
+        const int count = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;

+// Include row overhead in the partition weight, so empty rows are also balanced.
+// Whole rows retain the original floating-point summation order.
+void partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters, const index_t numRows,
+                   std::vector<index_t>& rowCuts, std::vector<index_t>& itemCuts) {
+    const size_t ranks = rowCuts.size() - 1;
+    const uint64_t totalWork = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[numRows]) + numRows;
+    for (size_t p = 1; p < ranks; ++p) {
+        const uint64_t target = totalWork * p / ranks;
+        // If there are enough rows, give every rank at least one.
+        const index_t first = rowCuts[p - 1] + (numRows >= ranks ? 1 : 0);
+        const index_t last = numRows >= ranks ? numRows - (ranks - p) : numRows;
+        index_t low = first;
+        index_t high = last;
+        while (low < high) {
+            const index_t mid = low + (high - low) / 2;
+            const uint64_t work = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid;
+            if (work < target) {
+                low = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                high = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        if (low > first) {
+            const uint64_t before = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[low - 1]) + low - 1;
+            const uint64_t after = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[low]) + low;
+            if (after >= target && target - before <= after - target) {
+                --low;
+            }
+        }
+        rowCuts[p] = low;
+    }
+    rowCuts[ranks] = numRows;
+    for (size_t p = 0; p <= ranks; ++p) {
+        itemCuts[p] = rowDelimiters[rowCuts[p]];
+    }
+}
+
+// Chunk transfers at MPI's int count/displacement limit. The normal case uses
+// one collective, while large CSR arrays do not overflow MPI counts.
+template <typename T>
+void broadcastVector(std::vector<T>& data, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < data.size();) {
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min(data.size() - offset, size_t{INT_MAX}));
+        MPI_Bcast(data.data() + offset, count, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset += count;
+    }
+}
+
+void sliceWindow(const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const uint64_t offset,
+                 const uint64_t end, std::vector<int>& counts, std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < counts.size(); ++p) {
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[p], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[p + 1], end);
+        counts[p] = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        displacements[p] = last > first ? static_cast<int>(first - offset) : 0;
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void scatterSlices(std::vector<T>& global, std::vector<T>& local,
+                   const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const int rank, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    const size_t ranks = cuts.size() - 1;
+    if (ranks == 1) {
+        local = std::move(global);
+        return;
+    }
+    local.resize(static_cast<size_t>(cuts[rank + 1]) - cuts[rank]);
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    T dummy{};
+    for (uint64_t offset = 0; offset < cuts.back();) {
+        const uint64_t end = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts.back(), offset + INT_MAX);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            sliceWindow(cuts, offset, end, counts, displacements);
+        }
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[rank], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[rank + 1], end);
+        const int count = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        T* receive = count ? local.data() + (first - cuts[rank]) : &dummy;
+        MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? global.data() + offset : nullptr, counts.data(),
+                     displacements.data(), datatype, receive, count, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset = end;
+    }
+    // Keep only the rank's CSR block after distribution, including on rank 0.
+    std::vector<T>().swap(global);
+}
+
+void gatherOutput(std::vector<double>& local, std::vector<double>& global,
+                  const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const int rank) {
+    const size_t ranks = cuts.size() - 1;
+    if (ranks == 1) {
+        global = std::move(local);
+        return;
+    }
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        global.resize(cuts.back());
+    }
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    double dummy = 0.0;
+    for (uint64_t offset = 0; offset < cuts.back();) {
+        const uint64_t end = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts.back(), offset + INT_MAX);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            sliceWindow(cuts, offset, end, counts, displacements);
+        }
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[rank], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[rank + 1], end);
+        const int count = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        const double* send = count ? local.data() + (first - cuts[rank]) : &dummy;
+        MPI_Gatherv(send, count, MPI_DOUBLE, rank == 0 ? global.data() + offset : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset = end;
+    }
+}
+
+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    errno = 0;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (text[0] == '-' || end == text || *end != '\0' || errno == ERANGE ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

 // Function: spmvCpu
 //

     }

+// Interleave independent row accumulations to hide addition latency. Each row
+// still visits its entries in CSR order, with the same rounding as spmvCpu.
+void spmvLocal(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,
+               const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {
+    // Short rows do not amortize the extra bookkeeping of interleaving.
+    if (dim < 4 || static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[dim] - rowDelimiters[0]) < 16ULL * dim) {
+        spmvCpu(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+        return;
+    }
+    index_t i = 0;
+    for (; dim - i >= 4; i += 4) {
+        index_t j0 = rowDelimiters[i];
+        index_t j1 = rowDelimiters[i + 1];
+        index_t j2 = rowDelimiters[i + 2];
+        index_t j3 = rowDelimiters[i + 3];
+        const index_t end0 = j1;
+        const index_t end1 = j2;
+        const index_t end2 = j3;
+        const index_t end3 = rowDelimiters[i + 4];
+        const index_t common = std::min(std::min(end0 - j0, end1 - j1),
+                                        std::min(end2 - j2, end3 - j3));
+        const index_t commonEnd = j0 + common;
+        double t0 = 0.0;
+        double t1 = 0.0;
+        double t2 = 0.0;
+        double t3 = 0.0;
+        for (; j0 < commonEnd; ++j0, ++j1, ++j2, ++j3) {
+            t0 += val[j0] * vec[cols[j0]];
+            t1 += val[j1] * vec[cols[j1]];
+            t2 += val[j2] * vec[cols[j2]];
+            t3 += val[j3] * vec[cols[j3]];
+        }
+        for (; j0 < end0; ++j0) t0 += val[j0] * vec[cols[j0]];
+        for (; j1 < end1; ++j1) t1 += val[j1] * vec[cols[j1]];
+        for (; j2 < end2; ++j2) t2 += val[j2] * vec[cols[j2]];
+        for (; j3 < end3; ++j3) t3 += val[j3] * vec[cols[j3]];
+        out[i] = t0;
+        out[i + 1] = t1;
+        out[i + 2] = t2;
+        out[i + 3] = t3;
+    }
+    if (i < dim) {
+        spmvCpu(val, cols, rowDelimiters + i, vec, dim - i, out + i);
+    }
+}
+
 // ****************************************************************************
 // Function: verifyResults
 //

+int main(int argc, char** argv) {
+    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
+    int rank = 0;
+    int ranks = 1;
+    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
+    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks);
+
+    int status = 1;
+    try {
+        status = runBenchmark(argc, argv, rank, ranks);
+    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
+        // A failure on one rank must not leave its peers blocked in collectives.
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", rank, error.what());
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+    }
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return status;
 }

+// Include row overhead in the partition weight, so empty rows are also balanced.
+// Whole rows retain the original floating-point summation order.
+void partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters, const index_t numRows,
+                   std::vector<index_t>& rowCuts, std::vector<index_t>& itemCuts) {
+    const size_t ranks = rowCuts.size() - 1;
+    const uint64_t totalWork = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[numRows]) + numRows;
+    for (size_t p = 1; p < ranks; ++p) {
+        const uint64_t target = totalWork * p / ranks;
+        // If there are enough rows, give every rank at least one.
+        const index_t first = rowCuts[p - 1] + (numRows >= ranks ? 1 : 0);
+        const index_t last = numRows >= ranks ? numRows - (ranks - p) : numRows;
+        index_t low = first;
+        index_t high = last;
+        while (low < high) {
+            const index_t mid = low + (high - low) / 2;
+            const uint64_t work = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid;
+            if (work < target) {
+                low = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                high = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        if (low > first) {
+            const uint64_t before = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[low - 1]) + low - 1;
+            const uint64_t after = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[low]) + low;
+            if (after >= target && target - before <= after - target) {
+                --low;
+            }
+        }
+        rowCuts[p] = low;
+    }
+    rowCuts[ranks] = numRows;
+    for (size_t p = 0; p <= ranks; ++p) {
+        itemCuts[p] = rowDelimiters[rowCuts[p]];
+    }
+}
+
+// Chunk transfers at MPI's int count/displacement limit. The normal case uses
+// one collective, while large CSR arrays do not overflow MPI counts.
+template <typename T>
+void broadcastVector(std::vector<T>& data, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < data.size();) {
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min(data.size() - offset, size_t{INT_MAX}));
+        MPI_Bcast(data.data() + offset, count, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset += count;
+    }
+}
+
+void sliceWindow(const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const uint64_t offset,
+                 const uint64_t end, std::vector<int>& counts, std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < counts.size(); ++p) {
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[p], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[p + 1], end);
+        counts[p] = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        displacements[p] = last > first ? static_cast<int>(first - offset) : 0;
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void scatterSlices(std::vector<T>& global, std::vector<T>& local,
+                   const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const int rank, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    const size_t ranks = cuts.size() - 1;
+    if (ranks == 1) {
+        local = std::move(global);
+        return;
+    }
+    local.resize(static_cast<size_t>(cuts[rank + 1]) - cuts[rank]);
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    T dummy{};
+    for (uint64_t offset = 0; offset < cuts.back();) {
+        const uint64_t end = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts.back(), offset + INT_MAX);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            sliceWindow(cuts, offset, end, counts, displacements);
+        }
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[rank], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[rank + 1], end);
+        const int count = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        T* receive = count ? local.data() + (first - cuts[rank]) : &dummy;
+        MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? global.data() + offset : nullptr, counts.data(),
+                     displacements.data(), datatype, receive, count, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset = end;
+    }
+    // Keep only the rank's CSR block after distribution, including on rank 0.
+    std::vector<T>().swap(global);
+}
+
+void gatherOutput(std::vector<double>& local, std::vector<double>& global,
+                  const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const int rank) {
+    const size_t ranks = cuts.size() - 1;
+    if (ranks == 1) {
+        global = std::move(local);
+        return;
+    }
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        global.resize(cuts.back());
+    }
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    double dummy = 0.0;
+    for (uint64_t offset = 0; offset < cuts.back();) {
+        const uint64_t end = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts.back(), offset + INT_MAX);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            sliceWindow(cuts, offset, end, counts, displacements);
+        }
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[rank], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[rank + 1], end);
+        const int count = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        const double* send = count ? local.data() + (first - cuts[rank]) : &dummy;
+        MPI_Gatherv(send, count, MPI_DOUBLE, rank == 0 ? global.data() + offset : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset = end;
+    }
+}
+
+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    errno = 0;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (text[0] == '-' || end == text || *end != '\0' || errno == ERANGE ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

 // ****************************************************************************
 // Function: spmvCpu

     }

+// Interleave independent row accumulations to hide addition latency. Each row
+// still visits its entries in CSR order, with the same rounding as spmvCpu.
+void spmvLocal(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,
+               const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {
+    // Short rows do not amortize the extra bookkeeping of interleaving.
+    if (dim < 4 || static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[dim] - rowDelimiters[0]) < 16ULL * dim) {
+        spmvCpu(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+        return;
+    }
+    index_t i = 0;
+    for (; dim - i >= 4; i += 4) {
+        index_t j0 = rowDelimiters[i];
+        index_t j1 = rowDelimiters[i + 1];
+        index_t j2 = rowDelimiters[i + 2];
+        index_t j3 = rowDelimiters[i + 3];
+        const index_t end0 = j1;
+        const index_t end1 = j2;
+        const index_t end2 = j3;
+        const index_t end3 = rowDelimiters[i + 4];
+        const index_t common = std::min(std::min(end0 - j0, end1 - j1),
+                                        std::min(end2 - j2, end3 - j3));
+        const index_t commonEnd = j0 + common;
+        double t0 = 0.0;
+        double t1 = 0.0;
+        double t2 = 0.0;
+        double t3 = 0.0;
+        for (; j0 < commonEnd; ++j0, ++j1, ++j2, ++j3) {
+            t0 += val[j0] * vec[cols[j0]];
+            t1 += val[j1] * vec[cols[j1]];
+            t2 += val[j2] * vec[cols[j2]];
+            t3 += val[j3] * vec[cols[j3]];
+        }
+        for (; j0 < end0; ++j0) t0 += val[j0] * vec[cols[j0]];
+        for (; j1 < end1; ++j1) t1 += val[j1] * vec[cols[j1]];
+        for (; j2 < end2; ++j2) t2 += val[j2] * vec[cols[j2]];
+        for (; j3 < end3; ++j3) t3 += val[j3] * vec[cols[j3]];
+        out[i] = t0;
+        out[i + 1] = t1;
+        out[i + 2] = t2;
+        out[i + 3] = t3;
+    }
+    if (i < dim) {
+        spmvCpu(val, cols, rowDelimiters + i, vec, dim - i, out + i);
+    }
+}
+

 // Function: verifyResults
 //

     printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");

+// Include row overhead in the partition weight, so empty rows are also balanced.
+// Whole rows retain the original floating-point summation order.
+void partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters, const index_t numRows,
+                   std::vector<index_t>& rowCuts, std::vector<index_t>& itemCuts) {
+    const size_t ranks = rowCuts.size() - 1;
+    const uint64_t totalWork = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[numRows]) + numRows;
+    for (size_t p = 1; p < ranks; ++p) {
+        const uint64_t target = totalWork * p / ranks;
+        // If there are enough rows, give every rank at least one.
+        const index_t first = rowCuts[p - 1] + (numRows >= ranks ? 1 : 0);
+        const index_t last = numRows >= ranks ? numRows - (ranks - p) : numRows;
+        index_t low = first;
+        index_t high = last;
+        while (low < high) {
+            const index_t mid = low + (high - low) / 2;
+            const uint64_t work = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid;
+            if (work < target) {
+                low = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                high = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        if (low > first) {
+            const uint64_t before = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[low - 1]) + low - 1;
+            const uint64_t after = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[low]) + low;
+            if (after >= target && target - before <= after - target) {
+                --low;
+            }
+        }
+        rowCuts[p] = low;
+    }
+    rowCuts[ranks] = numRows;
+    for (size_t p = 0; p <= ranks; ++p) {
+        itemCuts[p] = rowDelimiters[rowCuts[p]];
+    }
+}
+
+// Chunk transfers at MPI's int count/displacement limit. The normal case uses
+// one collective, while large CSR arrays do not overflow MPI counts.
+template <typename T>
+void broadcastVector(std::vector<T>& data, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < data.size();) {
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min(data.size() - offset, size_t{INT_MAX}));
+        MPI_Bcast(data.data() + offset, count, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset += count;
+    }
+}
+
+void sliceWindow(const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const uint64_t offset,
+                 const uint64_t end, std::vector<int>& counts, std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < counts.size(); ++p) {
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[p], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[p + 1], end);
+        counts[p] = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        displacements[p] = last > first ? static_cast<int>(first - offset) : 0;
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void scatterSlices(std::vector<T>& global, std::vector<T>& local,
+                   const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const int rank, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    const size_t ranks = cuts.size() - 1;
+    if (ranks == 1) {
+        local = std::move(global);
+        return;
+    }
+    local.resize(static_cast<size_t>(cuts[rank + 1]) - cuts[rank]);
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    T dummy{};
+    for (uint64_t offset = 0; offset < cuts.back();) {
+        const uint64_t end = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts.back(), offset + INT_MAX);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            sliceWindow(cuts, offset, end, counts, displacements);
+        }
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[rank], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[rank + 1], end);
+        const int count = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        T* receive = count ? local.data() + (first - cuts[rank]) : &dummy;
+        MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? global.data() + offset : nullptr, counts.data(),
+                     displacements.data(), datatype, receive, count, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset = end;
+    }
+    // Keep only the rank's CSR block after distribution, including on rank 0.
+    std::vector<T>().swap(global);
+}
+
+void gatherOutput(std::vector<double>& local, std::vector<double>& global,
+                  const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const int rank) {
+    const size_t ranks = cuts.size() - 1;
+    if (ranks == 1) {
+        global = std::move(local);
+        return;
+    }
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        global.resize(cuts.back());
+    }
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    double dummy = 0.0;
+    for (uint64_t offset = 0; offset < cuts.back();) {
+        const uint64_t end = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts.back(), offset + INT_MAX);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            sliceWindow(cuts, offset, end, counts, displacements);
+        }
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[rank], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[rank + 1], end);
+        const int count = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        const double* send = count ? local.data() + (first - cuts[rank]) : &dummy;
+        MPI_Gatherv(send, count, MPI_DOUBLE, rank == 0 ? global.data() + offset : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset = end;
+    }
+}
+
+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    errno = 0;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (text[0] == '-' || end == text || *end != '\0' || errno == ERANGE ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

     }

+// Interleave independent row accumulations to hide addition latency. Each row
+// still visits its entries in CSR order, with the same rounding as spmvCpu.
+void spmvLocal(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,
+               const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {
+    // Short rows do not amortize the extra bookkeeping of interleaving.
+    if (dim < 4 || static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[dim] - rowDelimiters[0]) < 16ULL * dim) {
+        spmvCpu(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+        return;
+    }
+    index_t i = 0;
+    for (; dim - i >= 4; i += 4) {
+        index_t j0 = rowDelimiters[i];
+        index_t j1 = rowDelimiters[i + 1];
+        index_t j2 = rowDelimiters[i + 2];
+        index_t j3 = rowDelimiters[i + 3];
+        const index_t end0 = j1;
+        const index_t end1 = j2;
+        const index_t end2 = j3;
+        const index_t end3 = rowDelimiters[i + 4];
+        const index_t common = std::min(std::min(end0 - j0, end1 - j1),
+                                        std::min(end2 - j2, end3 - j3));
+        const index_t commonEnd = j0 + common;
+        double t0 = 0.0;
+        double t1 = 0.0;
+        double t2 = 0.0;
+        double t3 = 0.0;
+        for (; j0 < commonEnd; ++j0, ++j1, ++j2, ++j3) {
+            t0 += val[j0] * vec[cols[j0]];
+            t1 += val[j1] * vec[cols[j1]];
+            t2 += val[j2] * vec[cols[j2]];
+            t3 += val[j3] * vec[cols[j3]];
+        }
+        for (; j0 < end0; ++j0) t0 += val[j0] * vec[cols[j0]];
+        for (; j1 < end1; ++j1) t1 += val[j1] * vec[cols[j1]];
+        for (; j2 < end2; ++j2) t2 += val[j2] * vec[cols[j2]];
+        for (; j3 < end3; ++j3) t3 += val[j3] * vec[cols[j3]];
+        out[i] = t0;
+        out[i + 1] = t1;
+        out[i + 2] = t2;
+        out[i + 3] = t3;
+    }
+    if (i < dim) {
+        spmvCpu(val, cols, rowDelimiters + i, vec, dim - i, out + i);
+    }
+}
+
 // ****************************************************************************
 // Function: verifyResults
 //

     printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");

+int main(int argc, char** argv) {
+    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
+    int rank = 0;
+    int ranks = 1;
+    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);

+        // A failure on one rank must not leave its peers blocked in collectives.
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", rank, error.what());

+// Include row overhead in the partition weight, so empty rows are also balanced.
+// Whole rows retain the original floating-point summation order.
+void partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters, const index_t numRows,
+                   std::vector<index_t>& rowCuts, std::vector<index_t>& itemCuts) {
+    const size_t ranks = rowCuts.size() - 1;
+    const uint64_t totalWork = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[numRows]) + numRows;
+    for (size_t p = 1; p < ranks; ++p) {
+        const uint64_t target = totalWork * p / ranks;
+        // If there are enough rows, give every rank at least one.
+        const index_t first = rowCuts[p - 1] + (numRows >= ranks ? 1 : 0);
+        const index_t last = numRows >= ranks ? numRows - (ranks - p) : numRows;
+        index_t low = first;
+        index_t high = last;
+        while (low < high) {
+            const index_t mid = low + (high - low) / 2;
+            const uint64_t work = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid;
+            if (work < target) {
+                low = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                high = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        if (low > first) {
+            const uint64_t before = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[low - 1]) + low - 1;
+            const uint64_t after = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[low]) + low;
+            if (after >= target && target - before <= after - target) {
+                --low;
+            }
+        }
+        rowCuts[p] = low;
+    }
+    rowCuts[ranks] = numRows;
+    for (size_t p = 0; p <= ranks; ++p) {
+        itemCuts[p] = rowDelimiters[rowCuts[p]];
+    }
+}
+
+// Chunk transfers at MPI's int count/displacement limit. The normal case uses
+// one collective, while large CSR arrays do not overflow MPI counts.
+template <typename T>
+void broadcastVector(std::vector<T>& data, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < data.size();) {
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min(data.size() - offset, size_t{INT_MAX}));
+        MPI_Bcast(data.data() + offset, count, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset += count;
+    }
+}
+
+void sliceWindow(const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const uint64_t offset,
+                 const uint64_t end, std::vector<int>& counts, std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < counts.size(); ++p) {
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[p], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[p + 1], end);
+        counts[p] = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        displacements[p] = last > first ? static_cast<int>(first - offset) : 0;
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void scatterSlices(std::vector<T>& global, std::vector<T>& local,
+                   const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const int rank, MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    const size_t ranks = cuts.size() - 1;
+    if (ranks == 1) {
+        local = std::move(global);
+        return;
+    }
+    local.resize(static_cast<size_t>(cuts[rank + 1]) - cuts[rank]);
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    T dummy{};
+    for (uint64_t offset = 0; offset < cuts.back();) {
+        const uint64_t end = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts.back(), offset + INT_MAX);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            sliceWindow(cuts, offset, end, counts, displacements);
+        }
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[rank], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[rank + 1], end);
+        const int count = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        T* receive = count ? local.data() + (first - cuts[rank]) : &dummy;
+        MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? global.data() + offset : nullptr, counts.data(),
+                     displacements.data(), datatype, receive, count, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset = end;
+    }
+    // Keep only the rank's CSR block after distribution, including on rank 0.
+    std::vector<T>().swap(global);
+}
+
+void gatherOutput(std::vector<double>& local, std::vector<double>& global,
+                  const std::vector<index_t>& cuts, const int rank) {
+    const size_t ranks = cuts.size() - 1;
+    if (ranks == 1) {
+        global = std::move(local);
+        return;
+    }
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        global.resize(cuts.back());
+    }
+    std::vector<int> counts(rank == 0 ? ranks : 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(counts.size());
+    double dummy = 0.0;
+    for (uint64_t offset = 0; offset < cuts.back();) {
+        const uint64_t end = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts.back(), offset + INT_MAX);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            sliceWindow(cuts, offset, end, counts, displacements);
+        }
+        const uint64_t first = std::max<uint64_t>(cuts[rank], offset);
+        const uint64_t last = std::min<uint64_t>(cuts[rank + 1], end);
+        const int count = last > first ? static_cast<int>(last - first) : 0;
+        const double* send = count ? local.data() + (first - cuts[rank]) : &dummy;
+        MPI_Gatherv(send, count, MPI_DOUBLE, rank == 0 ? global.data() + offset : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset = end;
+    }
+}
+
+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    errno = 0;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (text[0] == '-' || end == text || *end != '\0' || errno == ERANGE ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}
+
 // ****************************************************************************
 // Function: spmvCpu
 //
@@ -105,6 +241,52 @@
     }
 }
 
+// Interleave independent row accumulations to hide addition latency. Each row
+// still visits its entries in CSR order, with the same rounding as spmvCpu.
+void spmvLocal(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,
+               const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {
+    // Short rows do not amortize the extra bookkeeping of interleaving.
+    if (dim < 4 || static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[dim] - rowDelimiters[0]) < 16ULL * dim) {
+        spmvCpu(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+        return;
+    }
+    index_t i = 0;
+    for (; dim - i >= 4; i += 4) {
+        index_t j0 = rowDelimiters[i];
+        index_t j1 = rowDelimiters[i + 1];
+        index_t j2 = rowDelimiters[i + 2];
+        index_t j3 = rowDelimiters[i + 3];
+        const index_t end0 = j1;
+        const index_t end1 = j2;
+        const index_t end2 = j3;
+        const index_t end3 = rowDelimiters[i + 4];
+        const index_t common = std::min(std::min(end0 - j0, end1 - j1),
+                                        std::min(end2 - j2, end3 - j3));
+        const index_t commonEnd = j0 + common;
+        double t0 = 0.0;
+        double t1 = 0.0;
+        double t2 = 0.0;
+        double t3 = 0.0;
+        for (; j0 < commonEnd; ++j0, ++j1, ++j2, ++j3) {
+            t0 += val[j0] * vec[cols[j0]];

+        for (; j3 < end3; ++j3) t3 += val[j3] * vec[cols[j3]];
+        out[i] = t0;

+    }

+int main(int argc, char** argv) {
+    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
+    int rank = 0;
+    int ranks = 1;
+    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
+    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks);
+
+    int status = 1;
+    try {
+        status = runBenchmark(argc, argv, rank, ranks);
+    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
+        // A failure on one rank must not leave its peers blocked in collectives.
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", rank, error.what());
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+    }
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return status;
 }
